## [0.7.1](https://github.com/brandonocasey/rom-weaver/compare/v0.7.0...v0.7.1) (2026-07-23)


### ⚠ BREAKING CHANGES

* **bundle:** reset bundle format to v1
* **cli:** unify arg structs, filter enum, expect/assume tokens
* **webapp:** the React form components' workerThreads prop is now threads.
* rename manifests to bundles

## [0.6.7](https://github.com/brandonocasey/rom-weaver/compare/v0.6.6...v0.6.7) (2026-07-22)


### ⚠ BREAKING CHANGES

* **bundle:** reset bundle format to v1
* **cli:** unify arg structs, filter enum, expect/assume tokens
* **webapp:** the React form components' workerThreads prop is now threads.
* rename manifests to bundles

## [0.6.6](https://github.com/brandonocasey/rom-weaver/compare/v0.6.5...v0.6.6) (2026-07-22)


### ⚠ BREAKING CHANGES

* **bundle:** reset bundle format to v1
* **cli:** unify arg structs, filter enum, expect/assume tokens
* **webapp:** the React form components' workerThreads prop is now threads.
* rename manifests to bundles

## [0.6.5](https://github.com/brandonocasey/rom-weaver/compare/v0.6.4...v0.6.5) (2026-07-22)


### ⚠ BREAKING CHANGES

* **bundle:** reset bundle format to v1
* **cli:** unify arg structs, filter enum, expect/assume tokens
* **webapp:** the React form components' workerThreads prop is now threads.
* rename manifests to bundles

## [0.6.4](https://github.com/brandonocasey/rom-weaver/compare/v0.6.4...v0.6.4) (2026-07-22)


### ⚠ BREAKING CHANGES

* **bundle:** reset bundle format to v1
* **cli:** unify arg structs, filter enum, expect/assume tokens
* **webapp:** the React form components' workerThreads prop is now threads.
* rename manifests to bundles

## [0.6.4](https://github.com/brandonocasey/rom-weaver/compare/v0.6.3...v0.6.4) (2026-07-22)


### ⚠ BREAKING CHANGES

* **bundle:** reset bundle format to v1
* **cli:** unify arg structs, filter enum, expect/assume tokens
* **webapp:** the React form components' workerThreads prop is now threads.
* rename manifests to bundles

## [0.6.3](https://github.com/brandonocasey/rom-weaver/compare/v0.6.2...v0.6.3) (2026-07-22)


### ⚠ BREAKING CHANGES

* **bundle:** reset bundle format to v1
* **cli:** unify arg structs, filter enum, expect/assume tokens
* **webapp:** the React form components' workerThreads prop is now threads.
* rename manifests to bundles

## [0.6.2](https://github.com/brandonocasey/rom-weaver/compare/v0.6.2...v0.6.2) (2026-07-22)


### ⚠ BREAKING CHANGES

* **bundle:** reset bundle format to v1
* **cli:** unify arg structs, filter enum, expect/assume tokens
* **webapp:** the React form components' workerThreads prop is now threads.
* rename manifests to bundles

### Features

## [0.6.2](https://github.com/brandonocasey/rom-weaver/compare/v0.6.1...v0.6.2) (2026-07-21)


### ⚠ BREAKING CHANGES

* **bundle:** reset bundle format to v1
* **cli:** unify arg structs, filter enum, expect/assume tokens
* **webapp:** the React form components' workerThreads prop is now threads.
* rename manifests to bundles

### Features

## [0.6.0](https://github.com/brandonocasey/rom-weaver/compare/v0.5.0...v0.6.0) (2026-07-21)


### ⚠ BREAKING CHANGES

* **bundle:** reset bundle format to v1
* **cli:** unify arg structs, filter enum, expect/assume tokens
* **webapp:** the React form components' workerThreads prop is now threads.
